day man you're incredible you submit me

00:28:34 --> 00:28:40

back and realize that almost every

um

00:28:50 --> 00:28:56
literally 95 of what I teach the

00:28:55 --> 00:29:01
and it's my belief that every mental

aspect of competition

the most important which will be

confidence on stage is a direct result

skills

00:29:11 --> 00:29:18
people tend to see things like

00:29:17 --> 00:29:26
it is but it comes out of

00:29:20 --> 00:29:26
the performance of physical skills

all my life I've seen Sports

00:29:28 --> 00:29:34
psychologists try to

00:29:31 --> 00:29:36

non-physical means

artificial confidence into people

00:29:49 --> 00:29:53
I've never been impressed by this nor

have I you know have any kind of

00:29:52 --> 00:29:56
positive effect on on Athlete

00:29:54 --> 00:30:00
Performance

what I do see build confidence is the

00:29:59 --> 00:30:06

00:30:02 --> 00:30:09
skills and using them successfully under

hey're preparing for

once they get this down that's where

true confidence comes from confidence

00:30:15 --> 00:30:21
doesn't come from words it comes from

00:30:17 --> 00:30:23
accumulated skills which experience

00:30:20 --> 00:30:26
shows you have

00:30:22 --> 00:30:28
been responsible for successful

00:30:25 --> 00:30:30
performances in the past and if you

00:30:27 --> 00:30:31

confidence Rises

00:30:30 --> 00:30:35
o

00:30:31 --> 00:30:38
when it comes to the mental aspects of

competition I created a program where

veryone had us was given a set of

in the gym they accumulated those skills

for the accumulation of offensive skills

I like to have my athletes work with

athletes who are lesser than themselves

in ability

so that they start to

up

ighly competitive athletes at this

tart so they can see okay there is a

um

02:57:32 --> 02:57:37

80cc

um

um

so that people went into

inspiring

three

04:45:27 --> 04:45:36
till has the property of both

04:45:31 --> 04:45:38
ardness and suppleness

04:45:35 --> 04:45:41
the harder you make steel

04:45:37 --> 04:45:43
the better it's Edge retention becomes

04:45:40 --> 04:45:47

04:45:42 --> 04:45:48
this is good but it comes at a price

04:45:46 --> 04:45:50
the harder you make steel the more

04:45:48 --> 04:45:52
brittle it becomes

04:45:49 --> 04:45:56
and now that edge

04:45:51 --> 04:45:56
can be damaged easily

04:45:56 --> 04:46:02
so the solution is to make the steel

04:45:58 --> 04:46:04
softer more malleable that will prevent

04:46:01 --> 04:46:06
breakage of the blade and

04:46:03 --> 04:46:08
chipping of the edge

04:46:05 --> 04:46:11
but when you make the steel softer that

04:46:07 --> 04:46:15
comes at a price and that price is now

04:46:10 --> 04:46:16
the edge loses its uh its its sharpness

04:46:14 --> 04:46:18
very easily

04:46:15 --> 04:46:21
and so

04:46:17 --> 04:46:23
the riddle of Steel is how to

04:46:20 --> 04:46:26
work with these two to the greatest

04:46:22 --> 04:46:28
degree possible and create an edge which

04:46:25 --> 04:46:30
is hard enough to stay sharp for long

04:46:27 --> 04:46:34
periods of time but without making the

04:46:30 --> 04:46:35
steel so brittle that the blade overall

04:46:33 --> 04:46:38
is compromised
